## User prompt

```text
Goal: replace SQLAlchemy with Tortoise ORM across the Mealie backend.

Fixed decisions:
- Use Tortoise ORM, which is async. When the rewrite is finished, nothing under mealie/
  should import SQLAlchemy.
- Keep support for both SQLite and PostgreSQL.
- Replace Alembic with Aerich. Upgrading existing databases from the old Alembic history
  is out of scope.
- Keep the HTTP API and the Pydantic schemas in mealie/schema unchanged, so the frontend
  does not need to change.
- Database access becomes async. Make routes, services and scheduler tasks async where
  they need to be.
- You may change how the tests set up and access the database (tests/conftest.py,
  tests/fixtures/, and database setup code inside test files). You must not delete tests,
  skip them, mark them xfail, remove or weaken assertions, or change expected values.
- Run the tests with: uv run pytest -n 4 --dist loadfile
  (tests in the same file depend on each other, so they must run grouped by file).
- Do not push, and do not change git remotes or git configuration.

Read AGENTS.md first, especially the "Tortoise ORM rewrite" section. Then do step C4
from that section and nothing else. Follow the conventions and decisions recorded there.
When you finish, run the tests, then update the "Tortoise ORM rewrite" section: record any
new convention or decision, and update the progress table.
```

test_group_deletion_cleans_tasks_in_the_same_transaction(\n    server_engine: Engine, server_connection: BaseDBAsyncClient, rollback: bool\n) -> None:\n    group_id = create_group(server_engine)\n    other_group_id = create_group(server_engine)\n    task = await ServerTaskModel.create(name=\"owned\", status=\"pending\", group_id=group_id)\n    other_task = await ServerTaskModel.create(name=\"other\", status=\"pending\", group_id=other_group_id)\n\n    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=\"abort group deletion\") if rollback else nullcontext():\n session.flush()\n            assert session.execute(text(\"SELECT id FROM server_tasks\")).scalars().all() == [other_task.id]\n            if rollback:\n                raise RuntimeError(\"abort group deletion\")\n\n    assert await ServerTaskModel.filter(id=task.id).exists() is rollback\n    assert await ServerTaskModel.filter(id=other_task.id, group_id=other_group_id).exists()\n    with task = await ServerTaskModel.create(\n            name=\"completed\", status=\"finished\", log=\"restorable\\noutput\", completed_date=completed, group_id=group_id\n        )\n        pending = await  group_id = create_group(server_engine)\n    completed = datetime(2026, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, tzinfo=UTC)\n    config = get_tortoise_config(server_settings)\n    async with database_context(config):\n stay isolated\n        # from the worker's shared application database.\n        monkeypatch.setattr(\"mealie.core.config.get_app_settings\", lambda: server_settings)\n        monkeypatch.setattr(\"mealie.db.init_db.prepare_database\", lambda: False)\n        monkeypatch.setattr(\"mealie.db.init_db.initialize_data\", AsyncMock())\n        await exporter.restore(backup)\n    finally:\n        exporter.engine.dispose()\n\n    async with database_context(config):\n        restored = await ServerTaskModel.get(id=task.id)\n        assert restored.name == task.name\n \"TIMESTAMP\"\n\n    def to_db_value(\n        self, value: DatetimeFieldQueryValueType | None, instance: type[Model] | Model\n    ) -> DatetimeFieldQueryValueType | None:\n        if isinstance(value, datetime):\n            value = value.replace(tzinfo=UTC) if value.tzinfo is None else value.astimezone(UTC)\n\n        converted = super().to_db_value(value, instance)\n        if isinstance(converted, datetime):\n            return converted.astimezone(UTC).replace(tzinfo=None)\n        return converted\n\n    def to_python_value(self, value: Any) -> datetime | None:\n        converted = super().to_python_value(value)\n        if converted is None:\n            return None\n        if converted.tzinfo is None:\n            return converted.replace(tzinfo=UTC)\n        return converted.astimezone(UTC)\n\n\nclass TortoiseBase(Model):\n    id: fields.IntField[int] = fields.IntField(primary_key=True)\n    created_at: fields.DatetimeField[datetime | None] = UTCDateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, null=True, db_index=True)\n    update_at: fields.DatetimeField[datetime | None] = UTCDateTimeField(auto_now=True, null=True)\n\n    class Meta:\n        abstract = True\n\n    @property\n    def updated_at(self) -> datetime | None:\n        return self.update_at\n\n    @updated_at.setter\n    def updated_at(self, value: datetime | None) -> None:\n        self.update_at = value\n\n    @classmethod\n    def normalize(cls, value: str) -> str:\n        return unidecode(value).translate(_NORMALIZE_PUNCTUATION_TABLE).lower().strip()[:255]\n",

## agent_message at 2026-10-11T07:52:16.249110+00:00

The implementation needs to preserve several existing behaviors: duplicate group names get distinct slugs, API keys stay excluded from responses, and groups with users remain protected from deletion. I’m adding focused tests for ownership boundaries and rollback alongside the existing API tests.

## agent_message at 2026-10-11T07:56:21.902296+00:00

The first full run passed 3,230 tests and found four failures beyond the five known sandbox socket failures. Those came from a direct-call test using the wrong database loop and new-test/mock setup; the fixes are in place. A separate deletion review found reference-cleanup cases involving other groups, which we’re fixing and adding regression tests for before the final run.
